# 错误码

> **说明：** 本文档整理了各模块常见错误码及其含义，旨在帮助开发者快速定位问题，文档将会持续更新。

<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">219004</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">任务过期，创建的任务时间早于当前时间，任务执行失败，提示该文案。</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">计划库</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">飞行任务已过期，无法执行</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">任务过期</td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">219014</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">设备不属于该项目</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">计划库</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场不属于该项目，无法执行当前操作或执行飞行任务，请将机场迁回该项目或到机场所在项目重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场不属于该项目，权限隔离</td></tr>

<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">219031</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">异常错误</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">计划库</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">任务失败，飞行器处于远程控制</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;"></td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">219032</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">获取飞机属性失败，由于机场和飞机未对频或者舱内无飞机</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">计划库</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">飞行器开机失败或未连接，请检查飞行器是否在舱内，是否安装电池，机场和飞行器是否已对频，并尝试重启机场后重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;"></td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">219034</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">断点续飞任务创建超过三天未执行，任务过期失败</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">计划库</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">该断点续飞任务创建后已超过三天未执行，为保证飞行安全，任务自动过期</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;"></td></tr>

<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514113</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场与飞机有线连接建立失败（降落阶段/远程调试）</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场推杆与飞行器无法连接，请检查飞行器是否在舱内，推杆闭合时是否被卡住，充电连接器是否脏污或损坏</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">1、停机坪下充电连接线断裂<br/>2、起飞前检查充电连接器通讯异常<br/>3、飞机不在机场停机坪上<br/>4、推杆片簧金属片没有插入飞机内<br/>5、检查修复以上异常，重启机场</td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514114</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">获取飞机电源状态，命令超时，或返回码不为0</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">获取飞行器电源状态失败，请重启机场后重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;"></td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514116</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场繁忙，机场正在执行其他控制命令</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">无法执行当前操作，机场正在执行其他控制指令，请稍后重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">指令冲突</td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514117</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">检查舱盖状态失败</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">舱盖开启或关闭未到位，请重启机场后重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;"></td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514118</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">检查推杆状态失败</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">推杆展开或闭合未到位，请重启机场后重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;"></td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514120</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场和飞机SDR连接失败</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场与飞行器断连，请重启机场后重试或重新对频</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;"></td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514121</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">急停状态</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场急停按钮被按下，请释放急停按钮</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">急停按钮被按下</td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514122</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">获取飞机充电状态失败（获取充电状态失败，飞行任务可执行，影响充电和远程排障）</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">获取飞行器充电状态失败，请重启机场后重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;"></td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514123</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">低电量无法开机</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">飞行器电池电量过低无法开机</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;"></td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514124</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">获取电池信息失败</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">获取飞行器电池信息失败，无法执行飞行任务，请重启机场后重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">1、电池没插到位<br/>2、电池电量低，进入休眠状态</td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514134</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">大雨阻飞</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">雨量过大，机场无法执行飞行任务，请稍后重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">大雨无法飞行</td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514135</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">大风阻飞</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">风速过大，机场无法执行飞行任务，请稍后重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">大风无法飞行</td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514136</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">市电断开</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场供电断开，机场无法执行飞行任务，请恢复机场供电后重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">1、无市电输入<br/>2、ACDC损坏<br/>3、ACDC与主控模块连接线缆断开</td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514137</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">环境低温</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">环境温度过低于-20℃ (-4°F)，机场无法执行飞行任务，请稍后重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;"></td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514138</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">电池正在保养中</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">飞行器电池正在保养中，机场无法执行飞行任务，请等待保养结束后重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;"></td></tr>

<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514140</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">电池存储模式设置失败</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">飞行器电池无法执行保养指令，飞行器电池无需保养</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;"></td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514141</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">中心控制板链路异常</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场系统运行异常，请重启机场后重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">设备逻辑异常</td></tr>

<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514143</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">推杆未合拢或者闭合到位</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">推杆未闭合或闭合不到位，请稍后重试或重启机场后重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">1、停机坪与主控模块电源线连接不良<br/>2、推杆舵机损坏<br/>3、推杆被异物卡住<br/>4、检查修复以上异常后，重启机场</td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514144</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">舱盖未合拢或者闭合到位</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">舱盖未关闭或关闭不到位，请稍后重试或重启机场后重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;"></td></tr>
<tr><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">514145</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">处于现场调试无法作业</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场设备端</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">机场处于现场调试中，无法执行当前操作或执行飞行任务，请断开遥控器和机场的数据线连接后重试</td><td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;vertical-align:top;word-break:break-word;line-height:1.5;">处于现场调试无法作业</td></tr>
